.. _opcode-map:

Ventium x86 Opcode-Map Reference (auto-generated)
=================================================

.. note::
   This page is **generated** by ``tools/opcode_map/gen_opcode_map.py`` from
   ``tools/opcode_map/coverage_data.json`` and verified against
   ``rtl/core/core.sv`` on every Sphinx build (``.github/workflows/docs.yml``).
   Do not edit by hand — update the JSON and re-run the generator.

Complete one-byte (primary) and two-byte (``0F``-prefixed) x86 opcode maps as
decoded by the Ventium P54C core in **functional mode** (the KV260 board path,
``cycle_mode=0``). Reference model: ``qemu -cpu pentium`` (P54C: no MMX/SSE).
Out-of-scope opcodes HALT loudly (``d_unknown`` -> ``S_HALT``); they are never
silently mis-executed.

Each cell's hover/click callout shows the instruction's concise description,
courtesy of the `x86 Opcode and Instruction Reference <http://ref.x86asm.net/coder32.html>`_
(ref.x86asm.net, coder32 edition), followed by the Ventium decode status and RTL evidence.

-------------

.. raw:: html

   <style>
   .opc-legend{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:.4em .9em;margin:.6em 0 1.2em;font-size:.85em}
   .opc-legend .lg{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.4em}
   .opc-legend .sw{width:1.05em;height:1.05em;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid rgba(0,0,0,.25);display:inline-block}
   /* overflow:auto + a max-height makes the matrix its own scroll viewport so the
      sticky index row/column (below) stay frozen. The 1fr columns fill the full
      content width, so a wide display shows the whole map with no horizontal
      scrolling; only a narrow window (content < ~16x3.2em) falls back to scroll. */
   .opc-scroll{overflow:auto;max-height:85vh;margin:.4em 0 1.4em;padding-bottom:.4em}
   .opc-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:2.4em repeat(16,minmax(3.2em,1fr));gap:2px;font-family:var(--mono,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,Consolas,monospace)}
   .opc-h{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;font-size:.8em;color:#444;background:#eef0f2;border-radius:3px;padding:.2em 0}
   /* Freeze the index headers: the low-nibble row stays put on vertical scroll,
      the high-nibble column on horizontal scroll, the corner on both. Opaque
      backgrounds + z-index keep the scrolling cells from showing through. */
   .opc-colh{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:4}
   .opc-rowh{position:sticky;left:0;z-index:4}
   .opc-corner{position:sticky;top:0;left:0;z-index:6;background:#e3e6e9;border-radius:3px}
   .opc-cell{position:relative;cursor:default;border-radius:3px;padding:.25em .35em;min-height:2.7em;overflow:hidden;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:flex-start;gap:.02em;border:1px solid rgba(0,0,0,.12);transition:transform .05s ease,box-shadow .05s ease;outline:none}
   .opc-cell:hover,.opc-cell:focus,.opc-cell.opc-pin{transform:scale(1.04);box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.28);z-index:3}
   .opc-cell .opc-op{font-size:.6em;font-weight:700;opacity:.5;line-height:1.1;margin-bottom:.12em}
   .opc-cell .opc-m{font-size:.75em;font-weight:700;line-height:1.16;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
   .opc-cell .opc-a{font-size:.7em;line-height:1.14;color:#4a4a4a;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
   .opc-cell .opc-n{font-size:.62em;font-style:italic;color:#8a8a8a;line-height:1.1;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
   .opc-undefined .opc-m,.opc-undefined .opc-a{color:#888}
   .opc-implemented     {background:#e6f4ea;border-color:#9ccaa8}
   .opc-over-implemented{background:#e0f4f3;border-color:#88ccc6}
   .opc-partial         {background:#fff5da;border-color:#e6c463}
   .opc-system-only     {background:#e4eefb;border-color:#9bbde8}
   .opc-gap             {background:#fde7e7;border-color:#e6a3a3}
   .opc-prefix          {background:#f1e8f8;border-color:#c8a8e0}
   .opc-undefined       {background:#f4f4f4;border-color:#dadada;color:#888}
   .sw-implemented{background:#e6f4ea}.sw-over-implemented{background:#e0f4f3}.sw-partial{background:#fff5da}
   .sw-system-only{background:#e4eefb}.sw-gap{background:#fde7e7}.sw-prefix{background:#f1e8f8}.sw-undefined{background:#f4f4f4}
   #opc-tip{position:absolute;z-index:50;max-width:30em;background:#1e1e2a;color:#f4f4f6;border-radius:6px;padding:.6em .75em;font-size:.8rem;line-height:1.35;box-shadow:0 6px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.4);pointer-events:none;display:none}
   #opc-tip .t-op{font-family:var(--mono,Menlo,Consolas,monospace);font-weight:700;color:#ffd479}
   #opc-tip .t-badge{display:inline-block;margin-left:.5em;padding:.05em .5em;border-radius:10px;font-size:.78em;font-weight:700;color:#1e1e2a}
   #opc-tip .t-desc{margin:.4em 0 0;font-size:.95em}
   #opc-tip .t-detail{margin:.5em 0 0;font-size:.9em;opacity:.92}
   #opc-tip .t-lbl{display:inline-block;font-size:.82em;font-weight:700;color:#ffd479;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.03em;margin-right:.3em}
   #opc-tip .t-ev{margin:.4em 0 0;font-family:var(--mono,Menlo,Consolas,monospace);font-size:.84em;opacity:.62;white-space:pre-wrap}
   #opc-tip .b-implemented{background:#7fd49a}#opc-tip .b-over-implemented{background:#79ccc4}
   #opc-tip .b-partial{background:#f0cf6a}#opc-tip .b-system-only{background:#8fb6ec}
   #opc-tip .b-gap{background:#ef9a9a}#opc-tip .b-prefix{background:#cdaee6}#opc-tip .b-undefined{background:#cfcfcf}
   .opc-hint{font-size:.82em;color:#666;margin:.2em 0 .8em}

.. raw:: html

   <div class="opc-legend">
   <span class="lg"><span class="sw sw-implemented"></span><strong>implemented</strong> — Fully decoded and retired; behaviour QEMU-matched for the P54C model.</span>
   <span class="lg"><span class="sw sw-over-implemented"></span><strong>over-impl.</strong> — Decoded and retired even though the opcode post-dates P54C (P6/MMX/SSE/CET-era) -&gt; beyond the P54C ISA, but the Ventium core handles it rather than HALTing.</span>
   <span class="lg"><span class="sw sw-partial"></span><strong>partial</strong> — Decoded, but some ModRM/x87 sub-forms, modes, or value-ranges are gated off and HALT.</span>
   <span class="lg"><span class="sw sw-system-only"></span><strong>system-only</strong> — Decodes only in ``sys_mode``; user mode HALTs.</span>
   <span class="lg"><span class="sw sw-gap"></span><strong>gap</strong> — Architecturally-valid P54C opcode with no decode arm -&gt; HALTs. (Implementation target.)</span>
   <span class="lg"><span class="sw sw-prefix"></span><strong>prefix</strong> — Legacy/segment/operand prefix; consumed in the prefix loop.</span>
   <span class="lg"><span class="sw sw-undefined"></span><strong>undefined</strong> — Not a P54C instruction (P6/MMX/SSE/post-Pentium/reserved); HALTs.</span>
   </div>
   <p class="opc-hint">Hover (or tab to, or click to pin) any cell for what the instruction does, its Ventium decode status and sub-form notes, and the RTL evidence. Instruction descriptions are from <a href="http://ref.x86asm.net/coder32.html">ref.x86asm.net</a>. The same status glyphs (<code>I + ~ S X P .</code>) appear in the coverage table below.</p>

colour-coded by status; hover/focus/click for full details.

Descriptor type, CPL/RPL and table bounds are checked; presence is not a filter and queries never write the source or descriptor. Real/V86 and illegal LOCK queries raise #UD; unsupported user-mode forms retain the loud halt." data-evidence="decode_stages.sv:0F 00 SYS_VERR/SYS_VERW; shared S_DQUERY in core.sv/core_seg_ljmp.svh; verif/sys/tests/pverrw directed qualification. /0..3: core_exec.svh SYS_STR/SYS_SLDT and core_tss_priv.svh S_LTR (LLDT/LTR)"><span class="opc-op">00</span><span class="opc-m">GRP6</span><span class="opc-n">(SLDT/STR/LLDT/LTR/VERR/VERW r/m)</span></div>

System-context (boot path)." data-evidence="core.sv:1451-1463 (LGDT/LIDT/SGDT/SIDT arms; else d_unknown); exec core_fetch_decode.svh:406 (S_LGDT), :450 (S_SGDT)"><span class="opc-op">01</span><span class="opc-m">GRP7</span><span class="opc-n">(SGDT/SIDT/LGDT/LIDT/SMSW/LMSW/INVLPG)</span></div>

.. raw:: html

   <script>
   (function(){
     var tip=document.getElementById('opc-tip'); if(!tip) return;
     var pinned=null;
     function fill(c){
       var st=c.getAttribute('data-status');
       var h='<span class="t-op">'+c.getAttribute('data-op')+'</span>'
            +'<span class="t-badge b-'+st+'">'+st+'</span>'
            +'<div class="t-mnem"><strong>'+c.getAttribute('data-mnem')+'</strong></div>';
       var desc=c.getAttribute('data-desc'); if(desc) h+='<div class="t-desc">'+desc+'</div>';
       var d=c.getAttribute('data-detail'); if(d) h+='<div class="t-detail"><span class="t-lbl">Ventium</span> '+d+'</div>';
       var e=c.getAttribute('data-evidence'); if(e) h+='<div class="t-ev">'+e+'</div>';
       tip.innerHTML=h;
     }
     function place(c){
       tip.style.display='block';
       var r=c.getBoundingClientRect(), tr=tip.getBoundingClientRect();
       var sx=window.pageXOffset, sy=window.pageYOffset;
       var x=r.left+sx, y=r.bottom+sy+6;
       var maxx=sx+document.documentElement.clientWidth-tr.width-8;
       if(x>maxx) x=Math.max(sx+8,maxx);
       if(y+tr.height>sy+document.documentElement.clientHeight && r.top+sy-tr.height-6>sy)
         y=r.top+sy-tr.height-6;            // flip above if it would overflow the bottom
       tip.style.left=x+'px'; tip.style.top=y+'px';
     }
     function show(c){ if(pinned&&pinned!==c) return; fill(c); place(c); }
     function hide(){ if(pinned) return; tip.style.display='none'; }
     document.querySelectorAll('.opc-cell').forEach(function(c){
       c.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){show(c);});
       c.addEventListener('mouseleave',hide);
       c.addEventListener('focus',function(){show(c);});
       c.addEventListener('blur',hide);
       c.addEventListener('click',function(ev){
         ev.stopPropagation();
         if(pinned===c){pinned=null;c.classList.remove('opc-pin');tip.style.display='none';return;}
         if(pinned) pinned.classList.remove('opc-pin');
         pinned=c; c.classList.add('opc-pin'); fill(c); place(c);
       });
     });
     document.addEventListener('click',function(){
       if(pinned){pinned.classList.remove('opc-pin');pinned=null;tip.style.display='none';}
     });
   })();
   </script>
